SignatureAtlas

v6hg38

DNA methylation marker signatures for human cell types and lineages, derived from a 87-cell-type deep WGBS reference.

GitHub ·built with YAME

171signatures 102contrasts 87cell types 1,092WGBS regions · 31,327CpGs 11,885HM450 probes · 141signatures

Download v6 wg.bed hm450.bed defs.tsv reference_panel_samples.tsv

Signatures

Signature IDⓘSignature ID = <annotation><dir>
-: in-group hypomethylated; +: in-group hypermethylated.
Annotation names one cell type (Hepatocyte), a lineage (Epith) or a curated set (Brain_Vascular). _No<X> marks a lineage with X left out.
Rows nest under the smallest contrast whose in-group contains theirs.		In-group Out-groupⓘEvery other panel cell type. All contrasts in this release use the complement as the out-group. WG regionsⓘClick the count to list the regions.
Region name = <signature_id>.<gene>_<chr>_<firstCpG>
Gene: dominant gene within ±10 kb. firstCpG: 1-based C of the first selected CpG. Intergenic regions drop the gene.		 WG CpGsⓘCpGs in wg.bed. Each CpG is the 2-bp dinucleotide, 0-based begin, 1-based end (end1 − beg0 = 2). No CpG is in two regions; a CpG chosen by several signatures stays in the one with the widest in-vs-out β margin; an exact tie goes to the lower MRMP P-number (rebuilds after v6). HM450 probesⓘHM450 probes in hm450.bed. A signature is listed on HM450 only if it keeps ≥ 5 probes.

How a signature is made

  1. Recurrent methylation pattern. Public WGBS is pooled into 87 deep cell-type methylomes.
    • Loyfer: 143 samples → 31 cell types
    • Zhou: 34 samples → 34 cell types
    • BLUEPRINT: 100 samples → 8 cell types
    • Tian: 40 samples → 14 cell types
    At each CpG, the 87 cell types form a binary methylated/unmethylated pattern. The 10,000 most recurrent patterns are the MRMPs (P1…P10000).
  2. Region extraction. The CpGs of each MRMP are grouped into regions.
    • Clustering: CpGs within 100 CpGs of each other join a run. The 5 largest runs of ≥ 5 CpGs are kept, plus the 5 loci densest in HM450 probes.
    • Curation on real β: a CpG is kept if ≥ 90% of the hypo side has β < 0.4 and ≥ 80% of the hyper side has β ≥ 0.6.
    • Refinement: a CpG is kept only if every single out-group cell type stays separated (β smoothed over 5 CpGs).
    • Deduplication: overlapping regions merge. A CpG claimed by several signatures stays in the one with the widest β margin (the worst-case in-group vs out-group β difference at that CpG).
    • Tie-breaking: on an exact margin tie, the CpG goes to the signature from the more recurrent MRMP (lower P-number). P-numbers are fixed by the panel, so renaming a contrast never changes selection. This rule applies to rebuilds after v6. Through v6, ties went to the alphabetically first signature; this decided 4 CpGs (Neural+ over Neural_CNS+).
  3. Annotation. A curated contrast names a set of cell types (the in-group) against all others (the out-group). An MRMP that splits the panel exactly into that in-group and out-group becomes a signature. Each contrast gives up to two signatures: in-group hypomethylated (-) and hypermethylated (+).

Releases are git tags (v2…v6); file names carry no version.
